- **uberscan**: Whole-codebase read-only audit. Packs the repo into a fixed fleet of byte-balanced areas (default 8) and runs ONE multi-lens reviewer per area, plus an inline repo-global Semgrep + test-coverage pass. Aggregates findings into a markdown report and files deduped GitHub issues. Never writes code. Whole-repo by default; pass a path/glob to narrow. ([UberDevCorp/UberDev](https://github.com/UberDevCorp/UberDev/blob/HEAD/plugins/uberdev/commands/uberscan.md) · ★2)
- **ubersimplify**: Whole-codebase 3-lens simplification (Reuse/Quality/Efficiency). Packs the repo into a fixed fleet of byte-balanced areas (default 8), audits each area with ONE multi-lens code-simplifier, applies preserve-behavior fixes via code-fixer as one refactor: commit per area on a new branch, opens ONE PR for review, and files leftover blocker findings as GitHub issues. Whole-repo by default; pass a path/glob to narrow. --audit-only for a read-only scan. ([UberDevCorp/UberDev](https://github.com/UberDevCorp/UberDev/blob/HEAD/plugins/uberdev/commands/ubersimplify.md) · ★2)
- **uberthink**: Read-only cross-domain ideation engine. Fans an agent fleet over parallel islands to generate, gap-fill, combine, and falsify novel approaches across donor domains (SWE/gamedev/IT/math + far-field wildcards). Emits a ranked dossier with a moonshot lane and files top ideas as GitHub issues. ([UberDevCorp/UberDev](https://github.com/UberDevCorp/UberDev/blob/HEAD/plugins/uberdev/commands/uberthink.md) · ★2)
